+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "correlate radiosity");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(), state.deviceDistances,
+                         state.numTriangles * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "copy distances");
 }

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

 // Room Response Simulation State
 // ============================================================================

+    int* deviceTau = nullptr;
+
     size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };

+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&state.deviceTriangles, state.numTriangles * sizeof(DeviceTriangle)), "allocate triangles");

+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&state.deviceDistances, state.numTriangles * sizeof(float)), "allocate distances");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.deviceTriangles, deviceGeometry.data(),
+                         state.numTriangles * sizeof(DeviceTriangle), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "copy triangles");

 }

+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;

+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

     size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+    int* deviceTau = nullptr;
+
     size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
     size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
 };

+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

 // Room Response Simulation State
 // ============================================================================

+    int* deviceTau = nullptr;

+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.deviceTriangles, deviceGeometry.data(),
+                         state.numTriangles * sizeof(DeviceTriangle), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "copy triangles");

+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

 // Room Response Simulation State
 // ============================================================================

+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.deviceTriangles, deviceGeometry.data(),
+                         state.numTriangles * sizeof(DeviceTriangle), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "copy triangles");
+    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.deviceAreas, state.areas.data(),

+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}

+// The mesh is a convex icosphere. A segment joining points in the interiors of
+// two of its faces lies inside the room, so visibility is one for those pairs.
+// This lets each CUDA thread evaluate its pair without a divergent tree walk.
+struct DeviceTriangle {
+    float3 a, b, c, normal, center;
+};
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s failed: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ inline float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ inline float3 mul3(float3 a, float s) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s);
+}
+__device__ inline float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ inline float3 sampleTriangle(const DeviceTriangle& tri, float u, float v) {
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f - u; v = 1.0f - v; }
+    return add3(tri.a, add3(mul3(sub3(tri.b, tri.a), u),
+                            mul3(sub3(tri.c, tri.a), v)));
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const DeviceTriangle* triangles, const float* samples,
+                                  float* kij, int* tau, int n, size_t firstPair,
+                                  size_t batchCount) {
+    size_t localPair = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localPair >= batchCount) return;
+    size_t pair = firstPair + localPair;
+    int i = static_cast<int>(pair / n), j = static_cast<int>(pair % n);
+    if (i == j) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; tau[pair] = 0; return; }
+    DeviceTriangle a = triangles[i], b = triangles[j];
+    float3 centers = sub3(a.center, b.center);
+    tau[pair] = static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(centers, centers)) * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    if (dot3(a.normal, b.normal) > 0.99f) { kij[pair] = 0.0f; return; }
+
+    float result = 0.0f;
+    for (int ray = 0; ray < NUM_RAYS; ++ray) {
+        const float* values = samples + localPair * (NUM_RAYS * 4) + ray * 4;
+        float3 pI = sampleTriangle(a, values[0], values[1]);
+        float3 pJ = sampleTriangle(b, values[2], values[3]);
+        float3 v = sub3(pJ, pI);
+        float distSqr = dot3(v, v);
+        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
+        float inverseDistance = rsqrtf(distSqr);
+        float cosI = fmaxf(0.0f, dot3(v, a.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        float cosJ = fmaxf(0.0f, -dot3(v, b.normal) * inverseDistance);
+        if (cosI > 0.0f && cosJ > 0.0f)
+            result += (cosI * cosJ) / (PI * distSqr);
+    }
+    kij[pair] = result * INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+// One warp owns one receiver. Lanes read adjacent emitters, then reduce the
+// contributions before writing the current timestep's radiosity.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau,
+                                const float* areas, const float* radB,
+                                float* output, int n, int t, int source,
+                                int timeOff, float rho) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32;
+    int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    float sum = 0.0f;
+    for (int j = lane; j < n; j += 32) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        size_t pair = static_cast<size_t>(i) * n + j;
+        int delay = tau[pair];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        float factor = kij[pair];
+        if (factor <= 0.0f) continue;
+        float radiance = radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay) * n + j];
+        if (radiance > 0.0f)
+            sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], 1.0f) * radiance;
+    }
+    for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset >>= 1)
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    if (lane == 0)
+        output[i] = rho * sum + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void distancesKernel(const float* radB, float* distances,
+                                int n, int steps, int source) {
+    int i = blockIdx.x;
+    __shared__ float maxima[256];
+    __shared__ int best[256];
+    float localMax = 0.0f;
+    int localBest = 0;
+    for (int lag = threadIdx.x; lag < steps; lag += blockDim.x) {
+        float corr = 0.0f;
+        for (int tt = lag; tt < steps; ++tt)
+            corr += radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag) * n + source] *
+                    radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt) * n + i];
+        if (corr > localMax || (corr == localMax && lag < localBest)) {
+            localMax = corr; localBest = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    maxima[threadIdx.x] = localMax;
+    best[threadIdx.x] = localBest;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+            float other = maxima[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            int otherLag = best[threadIdx.x + stride];
+            if (other > maxima[threadIdx.x] ||
+                (other == maxima[threadIdx.x] && otherLag < best[threadIdx.x])) {
+                maxima[threadIdx.x] = other;
+                best[threadIdx.x] = otherLag;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * best[0];
+}
+
